Persists per-phase results to Supabase. Invoke as /deliver {group}/[{feature}/…]/{version} (e.g. /deliver listings/search/v2 → feature 'listings_search_v2'). ([jouvae/usvisoftball](https://github.com/jouvae/usvisoftball/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/deliver))
